#### Abstracts
##### **Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs**
2607.25959v1 by Fanfu Wei, Thibault Ehrhart, Raphaël Troncy

Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

摘要：維基百科和維基數據被廣泛用於信息獲取、LLM 預訓練和檢索增強生成。它們的知識深度相連，但分散在文本、表格和知識圖譜中。這引發了一個實際問題：當這些模態不一致時，我們如何檢測和解釋衝突？我們將這個問題研究為 \emph{模態級不一致性檢測}。我們首先介紹了一個跨模態知識不一致性的分類法，涵蓋信息粒度差異、直接衝突、時間變化和知識圖譜不完整性。然後，我們提出了 \textsc{Kontrast}，這是一個自動框架，利用 Text-to-SPARQL 和 LLM 推理來比較基於表格的答案與知識圖譜證據，並對產生的不一致性進行分類。對各種 Table-QA 數據集的實驗表明，跨模態不一致性是常見且具有信息價值的。它們不僅揭示了真正的知識衝突，還顯示了缺失的知識圖譜結構和時間不匹配，同時受到 Text-to-SPARQL 錯誤和噪聲的限制。我們的分析顯示，文本、表格和知識圖譜可以通過系統比較相互補充和修正。 \textsc{Kontrast} 提供了一個實用的工具，用於大規模知識審計，並為未來的跨模態知識一致性研究建立了一個基準。代碼和數據可在 https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST 獲得。

Counterfactual explanations identify minimal modifications that would change a model's predictions. Caption Studio is a transparency-first speech and audio intelligence platform that transforms spoken audio and video into structured, searchable content through automated transcription, speaker diarization, speech analytics, signal-level audio analysis, and subtitle generation. The system is built on a FastAPI backend with a real-time dashboard and adopts a three-layer architecture comprising (i) a transcription and diarization core based on Whisper-class automatic speech recognition and pyannote speaker diarization, (ii) an audio intelligence layer that extracts acoustic and linguistic features, including waveforms, spectrograms, pitch, speaking rate, silence, filler-word frequency, and sentiment, directly from the audio signal, and (iii) an integration layer that supports data export and downstream workflow integration. A principal contribution of this work is the transparency-first framework, in which every reported metric is explicitly identified as measured, derived, or unavailable, thereby improving the traceability, interpretability, and reliability of speech analytics. ##### **Do AI-Native Biotechs Need Departments? Benchmarking Company World Models for AI-Driven Drug Development**

The benchmark contains 45 retrospective public-information decision cases with strict time cutoffs, hidden outcomes, common schemas, automatic scoring, and blinded pairwise judging. Codex-only mechanistic ablations suggest that Revenue Room, Deal Room, and Approval Room carry useful work under the target objective. ##### **Semantic Primes as Explanans for Emotion in Large Language Models**
2607.18691v1 by Frank Xing

Progresses have been made on understanding emotion mechanisms of large language models (LLMs). However, how to explain emotion in LLMs, or even what constitutes good explanations, are less clear. Emotion representations, components, circuits are widely recoverable, but as explanations of a model's own computation they are circular; the emotion space dimensions tend to be arbitrary and non-terminating. A pressing question to ask is whether a more primitive set of internal variables does the work: the semantic primes of the Natural Semantic Metalanguage (NSM). Across four instruction-tuned LLMs (Llama-1B, Gemma-2B, Gemma-9B, OLMo-7B), experiments show that the NSM primes are (1) recoverable internal elements; and (2) on the reference model, intervening with a prime based direction controls emotion about three times as strongly, and twice as selectively, as the best appraisal based direction; and (3) the model treats a prime based explication as interchangeable with the corresponding emotion. These evidences suggest that NSM primes seem to be better explanans for emotion in LLMs than many alternative options according to scientific explanations criteria.

##### **Planning as Emergent Behavior in Reinforcement Learning with Relational Hidden States**
2607.18589v1 by Armin Sommer

Reinforcement learning is conventionally divided into model-based and model-free methods. In this taxonomy, model-based methods perform lookahead planning over a learned world model, whereas model-free methods learn a reactive state-action mapping. Recent work, however, has shown that planning can emerge from model-free reinforcement learning alone. The conditions under which this behavior emerges from a pure reward-maximization objective have so far remained unclear. In this paper, we present evidence that, in the observed cases, the hidden-state structure of the neural architecture is the deciding factor. We find that a network of relational hidden states, each anchored to an environment state and exchanging messages along learned relations, acquires a planning mechanism. These hidden states recover the environment's transition structure in their learned relations, and improve the policy at decision time by planning over the learned graph. In a matched control agent that must additionally discover which cells represent which states, no such binding arises, and no planning follows from it. We argue that this explains the observed phenomenon of emergent planning in model-free reinforcement learning and raises the question of how common such emergent planning might be more generally. Finally, we hypothesize that the discovered mechanism could describe how planning emerges from pure reward maximization in the human brain through a neural architectural prior.

摘要：強化學習通常分為基於模型和無模型的方法。在這一分類中，基於模型的方法在學習的世界模型上進行前瞻性規劃，而無模型的方法則學習反應性的狀態-行動映射。然而，最近的研究顯示，僅僅通過無模型強化學習也可以產生規劃。至今為止，這種行為從純獎勵最大化目標中出現的條件仍然不明確。在本文中，我們提供證據，顯示在觀察到的案例中，神經架構的隱藏狀態結構是決定性因素。我們發現，一個由關聯隱藏狀態組成的網絡，每個隱藏狀態都與環境狀態相連並沿著學習的關係交換信息，獲得了一種規劃機制。這些隱藏狀態在其學習的關係中恢復了環境的轉移結構，並通過在學習的圖上進行規劃來改善決策時的策略。在一個匹配的控制代理中，該代理還必須發現哪些單元代表哪些狀態，並未產生這樣的綁定，因此也沒有隨之而來的規劃。我們認為這解釋了在無模型強化學習中出現的規劃現象，並提出了這種出現的規劃在更一般情況下可能有多普遍的問題。最後，我們假設發現的機制可以描述規劃如何通過神經架構的先驗從純獎勵最大化中在人體大腦中出現。

##### **The Story Shapes the Agent: Narrative Priors in LLM Behavior**
2607.18566v1 by Yixuan Wang, James Lester, Shashank Srivastava

Persona prompting is widely used to steer LLM agent behavior, yet the narrative framing of a task can matter more than the assigned persona. We isolate this effect through structural isomorphism, constructing three text-based investigation games that share the same action space, stage progression, and resource constraints while varying only task narrative: disease investigation, IT troubleshooting, and murder mystery. Across 1,890 sessions spanning 3 models and 10 personas, we identify narrative priors: systematic action tendencies activated by a task's story framing, independent of its decision structure. Narrative priors explain 5-31x more behavioral variance than persona, are consistent across model architectures, and in two of three domains are negatively associated with task success. Persona effects that do transfer across narratives arise from behavioral anchors, persona descriptions whose language maps directly onto shared actions. Causal interventions confirm this: removing anchor words from a high-transfer persona reduces cross-narrative consistency by 95%. These results suggest that LLM behavior that survives narrative changes should be grounded in concrete actions rather than abstract descriptions.

摘要：地理空間基礎模型（GFMs）的基準越來越多地通過綜合分數對模型進行排名，但這樣的排名掩蓋了模型之間的差異：差距有多少是由架構造成的，多少是由解碼器容量造成的，以及多少是特定用例的產物？本研究通過對在歐洲航天局的$Φ$-lab下開發的兩個具有對比設計理念的GFMs進行受控比較，來解決這一差距：THOR引入了一種計算自適應架構，支持可變的補丁大小，並在其原生解析度下統一Sentinel-1、-2和-3數據；而TerraMind則是一種多模態生成GFM，通過雙尺度的標記/像素目標進行預訓練，實現任意對任意的跨模態生成（Thinking-in-Modalities），以在推理時推斷缺失的傳感器。我們並非報告單一的排行榜，而是調查這兩種架構實際上在補丁大小、解碼器複雜性、微調方案、輸入模態和模型規模等方面的差異，涵蓋十個用例，涉及多個領域的分割和回歸，包括氣候災害響應、甲烷洩漏檢測、雪監測或海冰制圖。我們發現，架構設計選擇——特別是補丁大小和解碼器類型——解釋的性能變異比模型身份本身更多，這兩個模型體現了互補的投資策略（TerraMind的預訓練時間規模與THOR的推理時間標記化），並且正確解釋結果需要數據集級別的特徵描述。最終的畫面不是單一的勝利者，而是一組假設和一種診斷性消融方法論，我們期望這能推廣到未來超越THOR和TerraMind的GFMs。

Adversarial instructions in a log, tool description, shared file, or peer-agent message may redirect the agent beyond the task the user assigned, even though every resulting command is authenticated and permitted for that account. To our knowledge, RELIABLE-BA is the first multi-engine binding affinity prediction framework to combine evidential fusion with context-dependent reliability, offering a principled path toward trustworthy AI-guided drug discovery. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA.

摘要：準確的蛋白質-配體結合親和力預測是計算藥物發現的核心，然而現代對接引擎經常存在分歧，卻未能指明應該信任哪個預測。共識評分和集成方法提高了平均準確性，但對所有預測一視同仁，缺乏可解釋的信心度量或不確定性分解，忽略了每對蛋白質-配體的化學背景。為了解決這一限制，我們引入了RELIABLE-BA（RELIABiLity-aware Evidential fusion for Binding Affinity），這是一個多引擎結合親和力預測的證據框架。我們的模型包含三個步驟：（1）通過正規-逆伽馬分佈將每個引擎建模為證據專家，（2）通過從分子背景中學習的可靠性來調整認知不確定性，同時保留每個專家的預測均值，以及（3）通過封閉形式的聚合融合專家，捕捉個體不確定性和引擎間的不一致性。在PDBBind和BDB2020+基準上的實驗顯示出具有競爭力的點預測，並顯著改善了不確定性校準，對SARS-CoV-2 Mpro數據集和5HT2A受體的額外驗證顯示了對臨床相關藥物靶點的適用性。關鍵是，這些不確定性估計使得可靠地篩選蛋白質-配體對成為可能，在僅保留高信心對的情況下將預測誤差降低了多達25%。據我們所知，RELIABLE-BA是第一個將證據融合與上下文相關的可靠性結合的多引擎結合親和力預測框架，為可信的AI引導藥物發現提供了一條原則性途徑。我們的代碼已公開在 https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA。

##### **Detection, Attribution, Narration: An End-to-End Pipeline for Explainable Money Mule Identification**
2607.17586v1 by Yuge Zhang, Yuanxing Zhang, Yichao Jin, Khairul Amsyar Mohd Razis, Nicholas Qi An Choo, Kai Yin Anders Wong, Xinyan Tang, Kenneth Zhu Ke, Wee Keong Dennis Lee, Jingyuan Zhao

Money mule accounts are critical facilitators of financial fraud, yet detecting them at scale remains challenging due to the heterogeneous nature of transactional and behavioural data. We present an end-to-end pipeline for customer-level mule detection comprising three stages: (1) a LightGBM classifier trained on 280 engineered features spanning transaction patterns, account demographics, network topology, and temporal behaviour; (2) a TreeSHAP attribution layer that decomposes each prediction into feature contributions; and (3) a large language model (LLM) module that converts SHAP attributions into analyst-facing natural-language narratives. We evaluate across three open-weight LLM families and assess explanation quality through analyst feedback. In a live production deployment, the system achieves a yield rate of 89%, up from 61% under the incumbent rule-based system, with monthly alert volume expanding from 211 to 302, reflecting broader true-positive coverage rather than increased noise. This corresponds to a 60% incremental adverse detection beyond existing review workflows, substantially outperforming the rule-based approach. Qualitative feedback from analysts indicates that LLM-generated narratives reduce cognitive load during alert triage. We further discuss implications of deploying LLM-augmented explainability in regulated financial environments.

摘要：金錢騙子帳戶是金融詐騙的重要促進者，但由於交易和行為數據的異質性，在大規模檢測它們仍然具有挑戰性。我們提出了一個端到端的客戶級騙子檢測管道，包含三個階段：(1) 一個基於280個工程特徵的LightGBM分類器，這些特徵涵蓋了交易模式、帳戶人口統計、網絡拓撲和時間行為；(2) 一個TreeSHAP歸因層，將每個預測分解為特徵貢獻；(3) 一個大型語言模型（LLM）模塊，將SHAP歸因轉換為面向分析師的自然語言敘述。我們在三個開放權重的LLM家族中進行評估，並通過分析師反饋評估解釋質量。在實時生產部署中，該系統的產出率達到89%，相比於現有的基於規則的系統的61%有所提升，每月警報量從211擴增至302，這反映了更廣泛的真陽性覆蓋，而不是噪音的增加。這相當於在現有審查工作流程之外的60%增量不利檢測，顯著優於基於規則的方法。分析師的定性反饋表明，LLM生成的敘述在警報篩選過程中減少了認知負擔。我們進一步討論在受監管的金融環境中部署LLM增強解釋能力的影響。

##### **Retain or Consolidate? Budget-Dependent Operator Selection for Language Agent Memory**
2607.17545v2 by Qingcan Kang, Mingyang Liu, Shixiong Kai, Kaichao Liang, Zhentao Tang, Yuqi Cui, Tao Zhong, Mingxuan Yuan

Language agents depend on memory across interactions. However, the limited context windows of large language models (LLMs) and their inference costs constrain how much memory can be used at once. Existing systems mainly follow two strategies: memory retention and memory consolidation. Retention keeps raw records and preserves exact details, but relevant evidence may not fit under a tight budget; consolidation compresses and combines records, improving coverage per token but risking the loss of query-critical details. Neither strategy is universally preferable. This raises two central questions: when should consolidation replace retention, and which operator -- Merge, Abstract, or Rewrite -- should be selected? We formalize this decision by decomposing each operator's utility into a coverage effect on evidence omitted by retention and a signed replacement effect on raw evidence that already fits. Their balance explains why the preferred action changes with relative budget pressure. We implement this mechanism with Offline Abstraction-Safety (OAS), a lightweight learner that estimates action utilities from pre-generation features with held-out harm calibration. The public LongMemEval and LoCoMo benchmarks show the same budget-dependent pattern. On LongMemEval, consolidation improves absolute accuracy by up to 48% under tight budgets, whereas retention is preferable under loose budgets; LoCoMo replicates this crossover at a smaller budget, consistent with its shorter evidence. On both datasets, cross-note abstraction and merging generally outperform local rewriting when compression is necessary.

摘要：語言代理在互動中依賴記憶。然而，大型語言模型（LLMs）有限的上下文窗口和推理成本限制了同時可以使用的記憶量。現有系統主要遵循兩種策略：記憶保留和記憶整合。保留保持原始記錄並保留確切細節，但相關證據可能無法在緊湊的預算下適應；整合壓縮並結合記錄，提高每個標記的覆蓋率，但可能會導致查詢關鍵細節的損失。這兩種策略都沒有普遍的優劣之分。這引發了兩個核心問題：何時應該用整合取代保留，以及應該選擇哪個操作符——合併、抽象還是重寫？我們通過將每個操作符的效用分解為對被保留所省略的證據的覆蓋效應和對已經適合的原始證據的簽名替代效應，來正式化這一決策。它們的平衡解釋了為什麼首選行動會隨著相對預算壓力而改變。我們通過離線抽象安全（OAS）實現這一機制，這是一個輕量級的學習者，從預生成特徵中估計行動效用，並進行保留傷害校準。公共的 LongMemEval 和 LoCoMo 基準顯示出相同的預算依賴模式。在 LongMemEval 上，整合在緊湊預算下將絕對準確度提高了最多 48%，而在寬鬆預算下則更適合保留；LoCoMo 在較小預算下複製了這一交叉，與其較短的證據一致。在這兩個數據集上，當壓縮是必要時，跨註釋抽象和合併通常優於本地重寫。

##### **CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models**
2607.17398v1 by Ruogu Chen, Weihua Xiao, Ramesh Karri, Jie Han

Analytical placers rely on differentiable objective functions to guide placement, typically combining intermediate surrogate metrics such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) and cell-density penalties. However, these placement-stage surrogates remain misaligned with downstream routed and timing quality. Prior work reduces this gap with human-designed terms or learned black-box surrogates, but the former requires expert retuning and the latter is difficult to explain, debug, or deploy in analytical placement flows. CoEvoP&R addresses these limitations with a large language model (LLM)-based framework that automatically evolves analytical placement objectives. At each generation, the prompt combines the restricted objective interface, baseline context, and archived prior candidates with routing-related feedback from placement, timing proxy, and routing tools. The LLM proposes readable differentiable objectives, which are embedded and validated in DREAMPlace, evaluated through a timing proxy and an actual router, and stored with their feedback to guide later generations. Across eight ChiP-Bench Nangate45 designs and three seeds, CoEvoP&R reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 16.9% and 36.7%, with gains of 0.70 ns in worst negative slack and a 912 ns reduction in total negative slack magnitude over native DREAMPlace. Across eight ICCAD 2015 Superblue designs, it reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 5.4% and 23.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git.

摘要：分析放置器依賴可微分的目標函數來指導放置，通常結合中間的替代指標，如半周長導線長度（HPWL）和單元密度懲罰。然而，這些放置階段的替代品與下游的路由和時序質量仍然不一致。先前的工作通過人為設計的項目或學習的黑箱替代品來縮小這一差距，但前者需要專家的重新調整，而後者則難以解釋、調試或在分析放置流程中部署。CoEvoP&R 通過基於大型語言模型（LLM）的框架來解決這些限制，自動演化分析放置目標。在每一代中，提示結合了受限的目標介面、基準上下文和存檔的先前候選者，以及來自放置、時序代理和路由工具的路由相關反饋。LLM 提出可讀的可微分目標，這些目標嵌入並在 DREAMPlace 中驗證，通過時序代理和實際路由器進行評估，並與其反饋一起存儲以指導後續世代。在八個 ChiP-Bench Nangate45 設計和三個隨機種子中，CoEvoP&R 將路由後的導線長度和擁堵減少了 16.9% 和 36.7%，在最壞的負時延中獲得了 0.70 ns 的改善，以及在總負時延幅度上減少了 912 ns，相較於原生的 DREAMPlace。在八個 ICCAD 2015 Superblue 設計中，它將路由後的導線長度和擁堵減少了 5.4% 和 23.2%。代碼可在 https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git 獲得。

#### Abstracts
##### **Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition**
2607.25961v1 by Podakanti Satyajith Chary, Barath Parthiban, Pranesh Velmurugan, Adeeba Khan, Nagarajan Ganapathy

Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

摘要：模糊性和猶豫性（A/H）是導致健康行為改變延遲或放棄的相互矛盾的情感狀態。 在視頻層面識別A/H是困難的，因為信號來自於面部、聲音、語言和身體模態之間及內部的不一致，並且在不同個體中表現不同。 提出的PRISM-AH（多模態模糊性/猶豫性識別的互動流預測推理）是一個將A/H視為隨時間展開的多模態衝突的框架。 凍結的視覺、音頻和文本編碼器被對齊到短時間窗口並傳遞到一個輕量級的流模型，該模型對跨模態不和諧進行評分，預測每個下一個窗口以揭示猶豫驚訝信號，發現行為原型，並基於參與者的元數據進行調整。 密集的窗口級註釋作為輔助目標監督模型，並且決策閾值經過宏觀F1的校準。 然後，知識引導的大型語言模型使用數據集的專家提示分類法對結構化證據進行推理，並且只有在驗證性能改善時才會進行後期融合。 在標記的公共測試分區525個視頻中，PRISM-AH達到了0.6133的宏觀F1，相比之下報告的零樣本基線為0.2827。 推理增益經過驗證可以從驗證轉移到更大的測試分區。

##### **A Cost-Effective Multimodal LLM Reasoning Framework for Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series**
2607.25947v1 by Frank Nie, Ethan B Liu, Yuan Zhu, Wei Fan, Jindong Han

Question answering (QA) over irregular clinical time series (ICTS) plays a pivotal role in a wide range of healthcare applications. Although recent multimodal time-series large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in general-purpose time-series QA, they remain poorly equipped to model the sparsity, asynchrony, and irregular sampling patterns of clinical observations. To fill this gap, we propose ClinPRISM, a cost-effective multimodal LLM reasoning framework for question answering over ICTS data. First, we devise an irregularity-aware multi-scale encoder to capture sparse clinical evidence at diverse temporal scales. Then, we propose a temporal evidence distiller to integrate representations across these scales and compress them into a small number of LLM-compatible tokens. Moreover, we introduce a progressive alignment strategy that sequentially aligns the irregular trajectories with the LLM's textual embedding space. To facilitate training, we construct 30,000 clinical time series paired with multi-scale descriptions, together with 41,000 instruction-tuning instances spanning 11 tasks. Using a 4-billion-parameter LLM backbone, ClinPRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the held-out evaluation benchmark while using only 16 time-series tokens and achieving an average inference latency of 0.15 seconds per question.

摘要：問題回答（QA）在不規則臨床時間序列（ICTS）中扮演著關鍵角色，廣泛應用於各種醫療保健應用中。儘管最近的多模態時間序列大型語言模型（LLMs）在通用時間序列QA中顯示出相當大的潛力，但它們在建模臨床觀察的稀疏性、非同步性和不規則取樣模式方面仍然能力不足。為填補這一空白，我們提出了ClinPRISM，一個具有成本效益的多模態LLM推理框架，用於對ICTS數據進行問題回答。首先，我們設計了一個不規則性感知的多尺度編碼器，以捕捉不同時間尺度上的稀疏臨床證據。然後，我們提出了一個時間證據提煉器，以整合這些尺度之間的表示並將其壓縮為少量的LLM兼容標記。此外，我們引入了一種漸進對齊策略，將不規則的軌跡與LLM的文本嵌入空間進行順序對齊。為了促進訓練，我們構建了30,000個臨床時間序列，並配有多尺度描述，還有41,000個跨越11個任務的指令調整實例。使用一個擁有40億參數的LLM主幹，ClinPRISM在保留的評估基準上達到了最先進的性能，同時僅使用16個時間序列標記，並實現了每個問題平均推理延遲0.15秒。

##### **Evaluating Multi-Turn Multimodal Diagnostic Reasoning on Challenging Real-World Clinical Cases**
2607.25933v1 by Rui Yang, Weihao Xuan, Yi Lin, Zhuhan Bao, Jonathan Chong Kai Liew, Matthew Yu Heng Wong, Nicolás Lescano, Nikita R. Paripati, Emily Ling-Lin Pai, Jiarui Liu, Heli Qi, Heng-Jui Chang, Benny Kai Guo Loo, Huitao Li, Kunyu Yu, Yufan Wang, Chuan Hong, Shijian Lu, Douglas Teodoro, Naoto Yokoya, Ross Koppel, Mona Diab, Hua Xu, David W. Bates, Nan Liu, Yifan Peng

Clinical diagnostic evaluation should not only assess whether models can provide correct diagnoses, but also reflect the realities of clinical practice, including progressive disclosure of multimodal information, dynamic updating of diagnostic hypotheses, and continuous refinement of clinical reasoning. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically rely on single-turn or isolated tasks, making it difficult to fully capture the complexity of real-world clinical diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we developed ClinMM-Bench, the largest multi-turn multimodal clinical diagnostic evaluation benchmark to date. ClinMM-Bench contains 1,089 challenging real-world clinical cases and 3,760 medical images across eight specialties. We systematically evaluated 15 representative MLLMs using a two-level evaluation framework that assessed both diagnostic accuracy and diagnostic reasoning quality. Results showed that proprietary models achieved the highest overall diagnostic accuracy, but the proportion of completely correct diagnoses remained limited across all models. In terms of diagnostic reasoning quality, current models can identify plausible diagnostic directions but still have considerable limitations in generating reliable diagnostic reasoning. Error analysis further identified five representative failure modes: information synthesis failure, knowledge mapping error, perception error, premature closure, and visual hallucination.

摘要：臨床診斷評估不僅應評估模型是否能提供正確的診斷，還應反映臨床實踐的現實，包括逐步披露多模態信息、動態更新診斷假設以及持續完善臨床推理。然而，現有的多模態大型語言模型（MLLMs）評估通常依賴單回合或孤立任務，這使得難以充分捕捉現實世界臨床診斷的複雜性。為了彌補這一差距，我們開發了ClinMM-Bench，迄今為止最大的多回合多模態臨床診斷評估基準。ClinMM-Bench包含1,089個具有挑戰性的現實世界臨床案例和3,760幅來自八個專科的醫學影像。我們使用一個兩級評估框架系統地評估了15個代表性的MLLMs，該框架評估了診斷準確性和診斷推理質量。結果顯示，專有模型達到了最高的整體診斷準確性，但所有模型的完全正確診斷比例仍然有限。在診斷推理質量方面，當前模型能夠識別合理的診斷方向，但在生成可靠的診斷推理方面仍然存在相當大的局限性。錯誤分析進一步確定了五種代表性的失敗模式：信息綜合失敗、知識映射錯誤、感知錯誤、過早結論和視覺幻覺。

##### **Depression Markers in Speech: An Approach based on Tract Variables Dynamics**
2607.25888v1 by Sahar Altalhi, Tanaya Guha, Alessandro Vinciarelli

This study identifies new depression biomarkers based on the dynamical properties of tract variables, which represent geometric features describing the configuration of the speech articulators. A key advantage of this approach lies in its ability to quantify aspects of the articulatory process that have not been previously explored in the context of depression, namely predictability, complexity, and randomness. These properties are respectively characterised using the Largest Lyapunov Exponent, the Correlation Dimension, and the Sample Entropy. Thorough experiments were conducted on the Androids Corpus, a publicly available dataset comprising 64 speakers diagnosed with depression by clinicians and 54 control speakers with no reported history of mental health conditions. The results indicate that the proposed biomarkers effectively discriminate between the depressed and control speakers, as evidenced by the high Cliffs delta values across both read and spontaneous speech.

摘要：這項研究基於軌道變數的動態特性識別出新的抑鬱生物標記，這些變數代表描述語音發音器配置的幾何特徵。這種方法的一個主要優勢在於它能夠量化在抑鬱背景下尚未被探索的發音過程的某些方面，即可預測性、複雜性和隨機性。這些特性分別使用最大李雅普諾夫指數、相關維度和樣本熵來表徵。對Androids Corpus進行了徹底的實驗，這是一個公開可用的數據集，包含64名由臨床醫生診斷為抑鬱的講者和54名沒有報告心理健康問題歷史的對照講者。結果表明，所提出的生物標記有效區分抑鬱講者和對照講者，這在讀取和自發語言中均顯示出高的Cliffs delta值。

##### **Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography**
2607.25748v1 by Hyunkyung Han, Min Jung Kim

Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

摘要：目標：概念瓶頸模型通過可解釋的中介變量進行路徑預測，其有效性通常通過這些變量的預測準確性來判斷。我們詢問這種判斷是否足夠，使用左心室容積作為從超聲心動圖視頻中估算射血分數的基本概念。<lb/>方法：對一個公開可用的超聲心動圖數據集訓練了一個視頻Transformer編碼器。心臟收縮末期和心臟舒張末期容積形成了一個概念層，從中以解析方式計算射血分數，且沒有剩餘路徑通向輸出。我們比較了僅在射血分數目標下的訓練與在毫升單位中對容積進行額外監督的訓練，並在1276個保留研究上進行評估。<lb/>結果：與直接回歸相比，概念瓶頸並未增加射血分數的誤差，平均絕對誤差為6.89對7.13。然而，在沒有容積監督的情況下，預測容積的分佈縮減至0.1毫升，而參考分佈為35.7和45.7毫升，雖然相關性部分得以保留。我們顯示這是由於目標的恆常性質：射血分數是一個比率，當兩個容積都重新縮放時不會改變，因此損失僅在比例上確定了概念層。以絕對單位進行監督將容積誤差從89.8毫升減少到25.8毫升，但射血分數誤差增加了0.4。<lb/>結論：僅僅依賴概念準確性可能隱藏一個不具物理尺度的概念層。<lb/>意義：臨床模型中的可解釋中介變量應根據訓練目標的恆常性結構進行驗證，而不僅僅是根據預測準確性。

##### **From Deterministic to Generative Deep Learning for Urban Air Quality Reconstruction from Sparse Observations**
2607.25687v1 by Abhishek A. Sabnis, Mihai Mitrea, Lya Lugon, Karine Sartelet, Marc Bocquet, Xiaoyuan Cheng, Shupeng Zhu, Sibo Cheng

Full-field reconstruction of air pollution is essential for evaluating pollution exposure and supporting public health decision-making. However, the complex interactions among pollutants, hard-to-predict weather patterns, and limited monitoring station coverage make this a complex task. We apply deep learning techniques to provide fast and accurate reconstructions from sparse observations of four key pollutants: NO2, O3, PM2.5 and PM10. Models are trained on full-field simulation data and evaluated on real-world observations collected from 9 to 28 monitoring stations in the city of Paris. We introduce a diffusion-based generative framework for multi-pollutant reconstruction and benchmark its performance against deterministic deep learning models. Despite noisy observations and strong spatial variability, the models achieve high structural similarity on simulated validation data and produce realistic spatial patterns on real-world observations, as indicated by power-spectrum analysis. ##### **Interaction Dynamics Modeling and Predictive Control for Safe Steerable Catheter--Tissue Interaction**

Safe steerable catheter control is fundamentally a problem of interaction dynamics: the tip must follow a planned motion, remain compliant against moving tissue, reject friction and hysteresis, and respect a clinically meaningful never-exceed contact-force bound. A predictive optimizer then regulates this interaction state subject to hard contact-force, tendon-force, and curvature constraints. An augmented Kalman filter compresses contact, friction, and modeling error into one sensor-free disturbance state, giving nominal offset-free regulation in free space while leaving force safety to the explicit constraint. The unconstrained and disturbance-free limit recovers classical catheter impedance as a special realization of the same interaction dynamics, rather than as the main design object. In a MuJoCo distributed-compliance simulation of an eight-link tendon-driven catheter, disturbance augmentation cuts free-space approach error by 90\%, and only the force-constrained predictive interaction-dynamics controller reconciles tracking with the 0.5\,N bound: the unconstrained controller drives contact force to 0.60\,N against a penetrating target, while the constrained one holds 0.47\,N at identical tracking. These results show that offset-free motion regulation and contact-force safety are coupled interaction-dynamics objectives, and that the explicit predictive constraint resolves their tension under stiff tissue contact. 摘要：肺部超聲波（LUS）是一種床邊工具，用於評估因心力衰竭或腎功能受損而有風險的患者的肺水腫。<lb/>然而，自動化的 LUS 分析仍然面臨挑戰，因為存在斑點噪聲、成像伪影和依賴操作員的獲取變異性。<lb/>在這項工作中，我們提出了一個深度學習框架，用於多類別 LUS 視頻分類，探索兩個組件：層次感知訓練和解剖引導學習。<lb/>從一個強大的基準開始，我們引入了層次訓練策略，然後引入胸膜線掩模監督，以引導模型注意力朝向解剖學相關區域。<lb/>我們使用來自 219 名患者的 1,886 個視頻的開放訪問數據集，研究四個臨床相關類別——健康、B 線、實變和混合 B 線與實變——並通過患者級別的五折交叉驗證進行評估。<lb/>結果顯示，層次感知訓練相較於平面分類改善了病理分離，而掩模引導的注意力監督達到了 65.7\% 的最高平均宏 F1 分數，並產生了更具局部化的注意力模式。<lb/>在外部 COVID-BLUeS 數據集上的轉移實驗進一步顯示了競爭性和參數高效的適應，同時保持了以胸膜為重點的注意力行為。<lb/>這些發現表明，將臨床結構化目標與解剖引導監督相結合是一種實用的方法，用於穩健且可解釋的 LUS 視頻分析。<lb/>代碼和模型實現可在 https://github.com/Alya-Almsouti/LUS-video-classification 獲得。

##### **Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning: Towards Task-Specific Zero-Shot Models in Healthcare**
2607.17508v1 by Sazan Mahbub, Caleb Ellington, Zhiyuan Li, Yixin Yang, Souvik Kundu, Ben Lengerich, Eric P. Xing

We introduce Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning (RAIL), a probabilistic meta-learning framework for zero-shot generation of task-specific interpretable models that synthesizes coefficient-space structure from natural-language task descriptions and a memory of previously learned task-specific predictors. RAIL retrieves related source tasks, transfers structure through coefficient space, and generates a new predictor in the original diagnostic-feature space, enabling zero-shot and few-shot clinical procedure prediction with feature-level explanations. Its probabilistic formulation provides uncertainty over retrieval, model coefficients, and predictions, supporting reliability-aware deployment: uncertain predictions or unstable explanations can be flagged for additional clinical review rather than treated as automatic decisions. This makes RAIL particularly suited for healthcare settings, where prediction tasks are highly long-tailed, new clinical targets arise frequently, and models must remain inspectable, uncertainty-aware, and compatible with human oversight. Across long-tailed clinical procedure prediction tasks, RAIL maintains reliable performance across data-availability regimes: it achieves 73.4% accuracy in the held-out zero-shot settings, where no supervised task-specific model can be trained, and remains near 73.2% accuracy in the extreme few-shot regime with only 2-4 examples, where supervised task-specific models perform close to chance. RAIL further benefits from clinically informed task representations and yields retrieval, uncertainty, and coefficient-level diagnostics that make model behavior more transparent. These results suggest a path toward scalable clinical prediction systems that can adapt to new tasks while preserving interpretability and reliability.

摘要：我們介紹了檢索增強可解釋學習（RAIL），這是一個概率元學習框架，用於零樣本生成特定任務的可解釋模型，該模型從自然語言任務描述和先前學習的特定任務預測器的記憶中綜合係數空間結構。<lb/>RAIL 檢索相關的源任務，通過係數空間轉移結構，並在原始診斷特徵空間中生成新的預測器，使得能夠進行零樣本和少樣本的臨床程序預測，並提供特徵層級的解釋。<lb/>其概率性表述提供了對檢索、模型係數和預測的不確定性，支持可靠性意識的部署：不確定的預測或不穩定的解釋可以被標記為需要額外的臨床審查，而不是被視為自動決策。<lb/>這使得 RAIL 特別適合於醫療環境，在這裡預測任務高度長尾，新臨床目標經常出現，並且模型必須保持可檢查、不確定性意識以及與人類監督相容。<lb/>在長尾臨床程序預測任務中，RAIL 在數據可用性模式下保持可靠的性能：在無法訓練任何監督特定任務模型的保留零樣本設置中，它實現了 73.4% 的準確率，並在極少樣本模式下（僅有 2-4 個示例）保持接近 73.2% 的準確率，而在該模式下，監督特定任務模型的表現接近隨機。<lb/>RAIL 進一步受益於臨床知情的任務表示，並產生檢索、不確定性和係數級診斷，使模型行為更加透明。<lb/>這些結果表明了一條通向可擴展的臨床預測系統的道路，該系統可以在保持可解釋性和可靠性的同時適應新任務。

##### **Auditing Question-Order Effects in Large Language Models with the QQ Equality: Mechanism Characterization and a Saturation Caveat**
2607.17219v1 by Pilsung Kang

Human survey respondents exhibit question-order effects that satisfy the QQ (quantum question) equality, an a priori, parameter-free prediction of the projective quantum question-order model. We develop the QQ equality into an audit criterion for sequential judgments of autoregressive large language models (LLMs). Theoretically, we characterize which mechanism classes satisfy it robustly: marginal-independent kernels satisfy QQ iff all four mismatch transition rates coincide (a class containing the 2D rank-1 projective model with a fixed measurement pair under state variation); a polarity- and position-dependent repetition family is characterized by an exact cross-symmetry condition with closed-form violations; QQ-satisfying behaviors are closed under order-matched mixing; and the rank-2 Contextuality-by-Default criterion translates into audit coordinates as $|\qQQ|\le\OSS$, where $\OSS$ (the order-sensitivity score) totals the order sensitivity of the two marginals. Methodologically, we develop a pre-specified, audit-logged pipeline applicable to any model exposing next-token log-probabilities; it combines worst-case robustness envelopes, sampling-consistency spot checks, full label counterbalancing, and a saturation diagnostic. Empirically, in a first-signal pilot on an open-weight instruction-tuned model under two framings, all pre-specified health gates passed, yet 17/18 and 7/8 item pairs, respectively, were saturated (near-deterministic), and no item was certified residually contextual. Forced-binary next-token log-probabilities were thus inadequate for distribution-level QQ audits under the tested model and prompting conditions; we recommend pre-specified saturation diagnostics whenever next-token distributions are treated as survey-response distributions.

摘要：人類調查受訪者顯示出滿足 QQ（量子問題）平等的問題順序效應，這是投影量子問題順序模型的一個先驗、無參數預測。我們將 QQ 平等發展為自回歸大型語言模型（LLMs）序列判斷的審核標準。在理論上，我們描述了哪些機制類別能夠穩健地滿足它：邊際獨立核當且僅當所有四個不匹配轉換率一致時滿足 QQ（這是一個包含在狀態變化下具有固定測量對的 2D 等級-1 投影模型的類別）；一個依賴極性和位置的重複家族由精確的交叉對稱條件及其封閉形式的違反特徵化；滿足 QQ 的行為在順序匹配混合下是封閉的；而等級-2 默認情境性標準轉換為審核坐標為 $|\qQQ|\le\OSS$，其中 $\OSS$（順序敏感性分數）總結了兩個邊際的順序敏感性。在方法論上，我們開發了一個預先指定的、審核記錄的管道，適用於任何暴露下一個標記對數概率的模型；它結合了最壞情況的穩健性包絡、抽樣一致性現場檢查、完整標籤平衡和飽和診斷。在實證上，在兩種框架下對一個開放權重的指令調整模型進行的首次信號試點中，所有預先指定的健康門檻均通過，但 17/18 和 7/8 的項目對分別達到飽和（近確定性），且沒有項目被認證為剩餘情境性。因此，在測試模型和提示條件下，強制二元下一個標記對數概率對於分佈級 QQ 審核是不足的；我們建議在將下一個標記分佈視為調查回應分佈時，預先指定飽和診斷。

##### **A Diagnostic Framework for AI Agent Behavior**
2607.17149v1 by Xichen Zhang, Yingjie Zhang, Tianshu Sun

AI agents increasingly act within the same clinical, political, scientific, and social systems that behavioral scientists study. Evaluating these systems requires source-level diagnosis: the same behavioral pattern may arise from an agent representational substrate or from the roles, objectives, interaction structures, and governance rules that shape its expression. This Perspective proposes a diagnostic framework for AI agent behavior: layer attribution. The foundational computational layer defines what behaviors are possible through architecture, memory, perception, attention, and representation. The behavioral modulation layer shapes how those capacities are expressed through identity, resources, objectives, social interaction, institutional constraints, and governance. The framework clarifies three consequences: surrogate validity is a model-task-layer relation, human-AI divergence provides diagnostic evidence, and governance requires source attribution before intervention. Treating AI agents as behavioral actors therefore requires evaluation methods that determine where behavior originates before deciding how to explain, validate, or govern it.

摘要：AI 代理人越來越多地在行為科學家研究的臨床、政治、科學和社會系統中運作。<lb/>評估這些系統需要源級診斷：相同的行為模式可能源自代理人的表徵基質，也可能源自塑造其表現的角色、目標、互動結構和治理規則。<lb/>本觀點提出了一個 AI 代理行為的診斷框架：層次歸因。<lb/>基礎計算層定義了通過架構、記憶、感知、注意力和表徵可能實現的行為。<lb/>行為調節層則塑造了這些能力如何通過身份、資源、目標、社會互動、制度約束和治理來表達。<lb/>該框架澄清了三個後果：代理有效性是模型-任務-層的關係，人類與 AI 的差異提供了診斷證據，治理在干預之前需要源歸因。<lb/>因此，將 AI 代理視為行為行為者需要評估方法，以確定行為的來源，然後再決定如何解釋、驗證或治理它。

#### Abstracts
##### **CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer**
2607.26023v1 by Ankang Yang, Jitao Zhao, Di Jin, Yuxiao Huang, Dongxiao He

Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

摘要：圖形基礎模型（GFMs）已成為跨圖形領域和任務轉移知識的有前景的範式。現實世界的圖形將節點與文本、圖像和其他模態相關聯，使得多模態圖形對於表示複雜實體和關係至關重要。此外，為每個新的圖形領域收集標籤和調整模型的成本高昂且往往不可行，這促使了零樣本轉移。不幸的是，在多模態圖形上進行零樣本轉移仍然未被充分探索。現有的基於GNN的圖形基礎模型通常需要下游適應，而基於LLM的圖形方法主要針對單一模態的圖形或單一領域內的任務。這種情況帶來了兩個主要挑戰。首先，模型必須從個別模態中概括知識，同時捕捉可轉移的跨模態關係。其次，在沒有目標領域微調的情況下，節點表示仍然與特定於領域的結構和特定於模態的特徵交織在一起，模糊了在未見領域中的共享概念。為了解決這些挑戰，我們提出了CHARM，一種具有層次上下文建模的多模態圖形基礎模型，用於零樣本轉移。CHARM用層次圖形上下文替換孤立的原始節點，這些上下文捕捉多模態語義和跨模態關係。這些上下文將特定於領域的節點模式映射到共享的高級概念，減少對目標領域監督或適應的依賴。一個模態感知的圖形上下文編碼器將多模態信息與圖形結構整合，並將生成的表示轉換為大型語言模型的圖形標記。實驗顯示在零樣本多模態圖形任務上有一致的改進。

##### **Does Runtime Topology Context Improve LLM-Generated Kubernetes Security Patches?**
2607.25995v1 by Farooq Shaikh

Kubernetes is central to the cloud-native ecosystem, orchestrating containerised workloads. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can automate cluster security remediation, generating configuration patches from Kubernetes Security Posture Management (KSPM) findings without human authoring. Such systems, however, prompt the model with each finding in isolation from the live service call graph, assuming general hardening knowledge suffices. This assumption breaks down whenever a patch must preserve a runtime service dependency invisible to the model: an otherwise compliant fix then carries a destructive functional blast radius, crashing downstream callers or silently severing call edges across the cluster. Whether live cluster context improves patch correctness has not been measured under controlled conditions across multiple dependency classes. We introduce KuTIE (Kubernetes Topology Intelligence Engine), which builds a live cluster context from Istio call edges, Trivy KSPM findings, and the service-account bindings a workload reads, and conditions LLM patch generation on it. It is evaluated on VulnCare, a purpose-built 36-deployment, four-namespace healthcare cluster with 31 injectable findings across seven dependency classes, each labelled by topology dependence against cluster ground truth. Across 248 trials, topology context raises topology-dependent patch correctness from 11.1% to 78.0% ($Δ= 0.669$), a gap that holds for every model and for six of seven classes, from credential and network-policy ($Δ= 0.95$) to role-based access control ($Δ= 0.31$); a topology-independent control exhibits no such effect ($Δ= 0.0$), isolating the result from generic prompt enrichment. Supplying the live service-call graph and the service-account bindings it exposes thus improves remediation of topology-dependent findings well beyond scanner-only context.

摘要：Kubernetes 是雲原生生態系統的核心，負責協調容器化工作負載。最近的研究表明，大型語言模型 (LLMs) 可以自動化叢集安全修復，根據 Kubernetes 安全姿態管理 (KSPM) 的發現生成配置修補，而無需人工編寫。然而，這些系統在每次發現時都會向模型提出問題，與實時服務調用圖隔離，假設一般的加固知識足夠。這一假設在修補必須保留模型無法看到的運行時服務依賴時會失效：一個原本合規的修復可能會帶來破壞性的功能影響，導致下游調用者崩潰或靜默中斷叢集中的調用邊緣。在受控條件下，是否實時叢集上下文能改善修補的正確性尚未被測量，且跨越多個依賴類別。我們介紹了 KuTIE（Kubernetes 拓撲智能引擎），它從 Istio 調用邊緣、Trivy KSPM 發現以及工作負載讀取的服務帳戶綁定構建實時叢集上下文，並以此為條件生成 LLM 修補。它在 VulnCare 上進行評估，這是一個專門構建的 36 部署、四個命名空間的醫療叢集，擁有 31 個可注入的發現，跨越七個依賴類別，每個類別均標記為相對於叢集真實情況的拓撲依賴。在 248 次試驗中，拓撲上下文將依賴於拓撲的修補正確性從 11.1% 提高到 78.0% ($Δ= 0.669$)，這一差距在每個模型和七個類別中的六個類別中均存在，從憑證和網絡策略 ($Δ= 0.95$) 到基於角色的訪問控制 ($Δ= 0.31$)；而一個獨立於拓撲的對照則未顯示出這種效果 ($Δ= 0.0$)，將結果與通用提示增強隔離開來。因此，提供實時服務調用圖及其暴露的服務帳戶綁定，顯著改善了對依賴於拓撲的發現的修復，遠超過僅依賴掃描器的上下文。

##### **MemLens: A Value-Aware Memory Management System with Interactive Analytics for LLM-based Agents**
2607.25992v1 by Shuyue Wei, Chang Liu, Zimu Zhou, Yongxin Tong, Lizhen Cui

Recently, memory management has become a key infrastructure for LLM-based agents, as it directly affects long-horizon reasoning, personalized responses, and knowledge reuse. However, existing LLM memory systems typically adopt a coarse-grained (utility-agnostic) manner that treats heterogeneous user-LLM interaction records uniformly, leading to redundant and low-impact records persisting in the memory repository. To address this challenge, we present MemLens, a value-aware memory management system that takes memory records as first-class data objects. MemLens provides an end-to-end interactive analytics dashboard that exposes the complete memory lifecycle, including Shapley-style memory evaluation, value-aware storage, and memory-assisted response. Through a study-copilot application, the system enables users to inspect memory values, visualize hierarchical memory structures, and compare various memory management strategies in terms of response quality, retrieval latency, and token consumption. Therefore, our MemLens can serve as an efficient, interpretable, and personalized long-term memory management system for LLM-based agents.

摘要：最近，記憶管理已成為基於LLM的代理的關鍵基礎設施，因為它直接影響長期推理、個性化回應和知識重用。<lb/>然而，現有的LLM記憶系統通常採用粗粒度（與效用無關）的方式，將異質的用戶-LLM互動記錄統一處理，導致冗餘且影響低的記錄持續存在於記憶庫中。<lb/>為了解決這一挑戰，我們提出了MemLens，一個重視價值的記憶管理系統，將記憶記錄視為一級數據對象。<lb/>MemLens提供了一個端到端的互動分析儀表板，展示了完整的記憶生命週期，包括Shapley風格的記憶評估、重視價值的存儲和記憶輔助回應。<lb/>通過一個學習助手應用，該系統使得用戶能夠檢查記憶值，視覺化層次記憶結構，並比較各種記憶管理策略在回應質量、檢索延遲和令牌消耗方面的表現。<lb/>因此，我們的MemLens可以作為一個高效、可解釋和個性化的長期記憶管理系統，適用於基於LLM的代理。

##### **Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition**
2607.25961v1 by Podakanti Satyajith Chary, Barath Parthiban, Pranesh Velmurugan, Adeeba Khan, Nagarajan Ganapathy

Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

摘要：模糊性和猶豫性（A/H）是導致健康行為改變延遲或放棄的相互矛盾的情感狀態。 在視頻層面識別A/H是困難的，因為信號來自於面部、聲音、語言和身體模態之間及內部的不一致，並且在不同個體中表現不同。 提出的PRISM-AH（多模態模糊性/猶豫性識別的互動流預測推理）是一個將A/H視為隨時間展開的多模態衝突的框架。 凍結的視覺、音頻和文本編碼器被對齊到短時間窗口並傳遞到一個輕量級的流模型，該模型對跨模態不和諧進行評分，預測每個下一個窗口以揭示猶豫驚訝信號，發現行為原型，並基於參與者的元數據進行調整。 密集的窗口級註釋作為輔助目標監督模型，並且決策閾值經過宏觀F1的校準。 然後，知識引導的大型語言模型使用數據集的專家提示分類法對結構化證據進行推理，並且只有在驗證性能改善時才會進行後期融合。 在標記的公共測試分區525個視頻中，PRISM-AH達到了0.6133的宏觀F1，相比之下報告的零樣本基線為0.2827。 推理增益經過驗證可以從驗證轉移到更大的測試分區。

##### **Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs**
2607.25959v1 by Fanfu Wei, Thibault Ehrhart, Raphaël Troncy

Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

We introduce Kimi K3, a 2.8T parameter Mixture-of-Experts model with 104 billion activated parameters, native vision capabilities, and a 1-million-token context window. At 2.8T scale, Kimi K3 is supported by infrastructure advances in multiple areas: algorithm-system co-design for KDA, perfectly balanced expert-parallel training with efficient memory management, million-token agentic RL with persistent rollout and sandbox states, and deployment innovations. While its overall performance still trails the most powerful proprietary models, namely Claude Fable 5 and GPT-5.6 Sol, Kimi K3 consistently outperforms other open and proprietary models evaluated in our suite. We release the full Kimi K3 model weights to facilitate future research and accelerate the broader deployment and adoption of frontier intelligence.

##### **TRACE-CTI: Auditable Post-Extraction Governance of TTP Claims with Knowledge Graphs**

Security Operations Centers increasingly rely on automated mapping of Cyber Threat Intelligence reports to MITRE ATT&CK, yet extractor outputs remain fallible and are often stored without the evidence, provenance, and validation history needed to decide whether an individual mapping should be trusted. We present TRACE- CTI, a post-extraction claim-governance framework that preserves run-level Predictions, aggregates them into configuration-level GraphAssertions, materializes setup-deduplicated corroboration as ConsensusAssertions, and exposes only GraphAssertions backed by policy-compliant validation grounds. The framework retains native evidence granularity, complete extraction provenance, versioned trust decisions, and non-destructive revocation history. We evaluate TRACE-CTI on two public CTI corpora comprising 65 reports and 5,303 sentences, using a controlled 2 x 3 matrix of retrievers and generator families, incrementally ingested across six GraphVersions. All setups are incorporated without schema modification; provenance paths remain complete, operational scopes remain disjoint, and every trusted GraphAssertion has an active qualifying validation ground. Cross-generator-family setup pairs exhibit greater output diversity than same-family pairs. At the final graph state, increasing setup support from k >= 1 to six-setup unanimity raises gold-aligned precision from 25.3% to 90.6%, while recall decreases from 88.2% to 16.3%. The graph also directly answers seven questions about provenance, trust, versioning, dependency, disagreement, and review-queue that the evaluated minimal flat output cannot fully answer without enrichment or reprocessing. These results support explicit, auditable governance of extracted TTP claims; the observed corroboration trajectory is descriptive and does not establish statistical independence or a causal model-family effect.

摘要：安全運營中心越來越依賴將網路威脅情報報告自動映射到MITRE ATT&CK，然而提取器的輸出仍然存在錯誤，且通常在沒有證據、來源和驗證歷史的情況下儲存，這些都是決定個別映射是否值得信賴所需的。我們提出了TRACE-CTI，一個後提取的聲明治理框架，該框架保留了運行級別的預測，將其聚合為配置級別的圖形聲明，將設置去重的證實具體化為共識聲明，並僅暴露由符合政策的驗證依據支持的圖形聲明。該框架保留了原生證據的粒度、完整的提取來源、版本化的信任決策和非破壞性的撤銷歷史。我們在兩個公共CTI語料庫上評估TRACE-CTI，這些語料庫包含65份報告和5,303句子，使用一個受控的2 x 3檢索器和生成器系列矩陣，逐步納入六個圖形版本。所有設置均在不修改架構的情況下納入；來源路徑保持完整，操作範圍保持不交疊，每個受信的圖形聲明都有一個有效的合格驗證依據。跨生成器系列的設置對比展示了比同系列對比更大的輸出多樣性。在最終圖形狀態下，將設置支持從k >= 1增加到六個設置的一致性，將金標準精度從25.3%提高到90.6%，而召回率則從88.2%下降到16.3%。該圖形還直接回答了七個關於來源、信任、版本控制、依賴性、分歧和審查隊列的問題，而被評估的最小平面輸出在沒有增強或重新處理的情況下無法完全回答這些問題。這些結果支持對提取的TTP聲明進行明確、可審計的治理；觀察到的證實軌跡是描述性的，並未建立統計獨立性或因果模型系列效應。

##### **LOCKS: Page-Local Compact Key Summaries for Efficient Long-Context Decoding**
2607.24555v1 by Junsung Hwang

Serving large language models at long context is bottlenecked by the key-value (KV) cache, which is read in full at every decode step. Attention keys are locally low-rank though globally high-rank: shared low-rank bases discard page-specific directions that a page's own compact basis retains. LOCKS gives every page its own spectral summary (resident, about a tenth the cache's size), reconstructs within-page logits, estimates each page's attention mass by log-sum-exp, and attends only the top pages; selection itself reads no candidate keys or values. Selecting on this summary alone stays within about a point of the full cache on long-document QA (LongBench-v1), tracks the read-every-key oracle on retrieval-dense RULER down to the smallest budgets, and shows its largest margins on long-form reasoning (AIME26, MATH-500), where baseline selectors collapse. At its shipped $2048$-token budget LOCKS matches FullKV aggregate quality at $100$K$+$ context while attending about $2\%$ of the tokens, and halves per-token decode latency ($2.0\times$ at $1$M tokens) against dense attention. LOCKS ships as a drop-in plugin for unmodified vLLM, with batched decode running in full CUDA graphs.

摘要：大型語言模型在長上下文下的服務受到鍵值（KV）快取的瓶頸，因為在每個解碼步驟中都需要完整讀取。注意力鍵在局部上是低秩的，但在全局上是高秩的：共享的低秩基底會丟棄頁面特定的方向，而頁面自己的緊湊基底則保留這些方向。LOCKS 為每個頁面提供自己的光譜摘要（常駐，約為快取大小的十分之一），在頁面內重建 logits，通過對數和指數估計每個頁面的注意力質量，並只關注前幾個頁面；選擇本身不讀取任何候選鍵或值。僅根據這個摘要進行選擇在長文檔 QA（LongBench-v1）中保持在完整快取的約一個點內，在檢索密集的 RULER 上追蹤每個鍵的讀取預言，直到最小預算，並在長篇推理（AIME26，MATH-500）中顯示出最大的優勢，基準選擇器在此崩潰。在其發貨的 $2048$-token 預算下，LOCKS 在 $100$K$+$ 上下文中匹配 FullKV 的總體質量，同時只關注約 $2\%$ 的標記，並將每標記的解碼延遲減半（在 $1$M 標記時為 $2.0\times$）相對於密集注意力。LOCKS 作為未修改的 vLLM 的即插即用插件發貨，批量解碼在完整的 CUDA 圖中運行。

##### **LLM-Assisted Ontology Engineering and Construction of a French Legal Knowledge Graph**
2607.24551v1 by G{é}nesis Montenegro, Mokhtar Boumedyen Billami, Catherine Faron, Fabien Gandon, Pierre Monnin

Maintenance regulations are complex legal texts that are difficult to exploit when addressing a specific case and challenging to integrate into operational systems. This paper presents a two-stage LLM-assisted workflow for French maintenance regulations: ontology engineering from a SEMLEG-based core ontology, followed by construction of an ontology-grounded French legal knowledge graph. The first stage consists in the open extraction of typed entities and triples from a stratified corpus sample, the normalization of labels through embedding-based fusion, and the induction of candidate object properties with their signature (domain and range). The second stage uses the resulting ontology to guide the closed extraction of triples and RDF graph construction over the full corpus. Social media and search delivered enormous gains on every familiar metric and still triggered a legitimacy crisis, because publics questioned who authorized a handful of firms to set the rules of speech, visibility, and knowledge. It is possible to build a benevolent AI and still face a political crisis over its authority.

Thick legality supplies what form cannot: public authorship of the rules that bind.

摘要：AI 系統已經在治理。它們對言論進行排名並分配注意力，過濾申請者並對主張進行分類。AI 治理的主導框架，即對齊，詢問這些系統是否安全地追求正確的目標。它無法回答一個先前的問題：這些目標是以什麼權利被設定和執行的？<lb/>

接下來是三個可移植的原則。整合將有影響的 AI 規則設定放置在一個政治體已經視為權威的場所。熟悉性以當地可信的形式呈現規則和理由。爭議保證了真實補救措施的可信二次審視。

Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Its 1,080 paired CVRP, VRPTW, TDVRP and TDVRPTW instances combine real road geometries with controlled synthetic demands, time windows and congestion. Every instance carries a checker-validated best-known solution. #### Abstracts
##### **$π\mathbf{R}^2$: Reactive Real-time Flow Policies**
2607.26055v1 by Sungjae Park, Shubham Tulsiani

Generalist manipulation policies increasingly take the form of action-chunking flow policies built on large pretrained backbones. Such chunks run open-loop, so the policy cannot react to sensory input arriving mid-execution, sacrificing \emph{reactivity}. Replanning more often would restore it, but the perception-to-action pipeline (a large backbone plus multiple denoising steps) is too slow: this \emph{latency} forbids frequent replanning and leaves committed actions stale, making such policies ill-suited for dynamic, closed-loop control. We present $π\mathbf{R}^2$, which makes these policies reactive and real-time while retaining large backbones, expressive multi-modal policies, and multi-action prediction. Built on the per-position noise schedule of diffusion forcing, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ contributes two ideas. First, it splits conditioning into a fast channel (proprioception, fresh every tick) and an asynchronously updated slow channel (vision-language features), so the policy reacts to proprioception within a chunk while tolerating stale vision. Second, a latency-adaptive flow schedule treats in-flight actions as inpainting conditioning and emits actions in one denoising step per call, letting one trained model adapt to varying hardware latency. Requiring minimal modification to existing architectures, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ can be finetuned from a pretrained policy: applied to GR00T-N1.7 on a real xArm6+XHand platform, it replans closed-loop roughly $4\times$ faster than the base policy (~$25$Hz on an A5000 GPU), acting on a fresh observation every $40$ms. Across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ improves the success rate by up to $23\%$ in simulation and $30\%$ in the real world over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

摘要：一般化的操控政策越來越多地採用基於大型預訓練骨幹的動作分塊流政策。這些塊以開環方式運行，因此政策無法對執行過程中到達的感官輸入做出反應，犧牲了\emph{反應性}。更頻繁地重新規劃將恢復反應性，但感知到行動的管道（大型骨幹加上多個去噪步驟）太慢：這種\emph{延遲}禁止了頻繁的重新規劃，並使已承諾的行動變得過時，使這些政策不適合動態的閉環控制。我們提出了$π\mathbf{R}^2$，它在保留大型骨幹、表達性多模態政策和多動作預測的同時，使這些政策具備反應性和實時性。基於擴散強迫的每位置噪聲計劃，$π\mathbf{R}^2$提出了兩個想法。首先，它將條件分為快速通道（本體感知，每次更新）和異步更新的慢通道（視覺-語言特徵），因此政策在一個塊內對本體感知做出反應，同時容忍過時的視覺。其次，一種延遲自適應流計劃將進行中的行動視為修補條件，並在每次調用中以一步去噪的方式發出行動，讓一個訓練過的模型適應不同的硬體延遲。$π\mathbf{R}^2$對現有架構的修改要求最小，可以從預訓練的政策進行微調：應用於真實的xArm6+XHand平台上的GR00T-N1.7，它的閉環重新規劃速度約為基礎政策的$4\times$（在A5000 GPU上約為$25$Hz），每$40$毫秒對新觀察進行操作。在模擬和現實世界的操控任務中，$π\mathbf{R}^2$在模擬中提高了成功率高達$23\%$，在現實世界中提高了$30\%$，超過了最強基準。項目頁面：https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

##### **Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?**
2607.26041v1 by Abhishek Pillai, Samir Kumar Nayak, Yuan Chen

Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

摘要：電腦使用代理 (CUAs) 越來越多地通過桌面 GUI 來完成長期任務。當前的基準主要測量最終任務的成功或單幀的基礎。這兩者都無法確定模型是否能重建由行動產生的因果、任務相關的轉變——這對於拒絕過時的觀察、驗證進展和從失敗中恢復至關重要。這是困難的，因為推斷、遠程輸入、應用程序渲染和截圖捕獲是異步的：下一個觀察可能會延遲、被遮擋、瞬時或無關，然後被誤讀為進展並帶入後續計劃。我們引入了 Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，這是一個離線步驟級基準，包含 2,013 個經過人類驗證的實例，來自於約 15 個應用程序和 50 個任務領域的全新多應用 Linux 軌跡。DDB 軌跡針對 3 個失敗維度——狀態驗證、來源追蹤和上下文感知控制——通過 2 個互補任務：463 個 3 幀時間排序實例，其中 105 個包含跨軌跡的誘餌，以及 1,550 個標記自 5 個行動及其有效載荷的前後對。 我們在 32 個排序和 16 個單一行動設置中評估了 8 個封閉和開源模型系列，觀察到一致的差距。排序仍然未飽和：最佳非誘餌和誘餌的精確匹配率分別為 65.1% 和 65.7%。任務上下文將誘餌識別的準確率提高了 6.9 個百分點，但將非誘餌的精確匹配降低了 2.2 個百分點；錯誤分析顯示了對呈現的 A-B-C 順序的系統性複製。單一行動的結果顯示，推斷行動類別比定位更困難：點擊 F1 的準確率為 0.96，而拖動為 0.76，而被識別的拖動通常定位良好。因此，DDB 通過填補 GUI 基礎和最終任務成功之間缺失的診斷層，補充了端到端基準，從而促進了對桌面 CUA 驗證、可靠性和恢復的有針對性的改進。

##### **Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment**

We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. 摘要：圖神經網絡（GNNs）和大型語言模型（LLMs）各自通過建模結構和語義信號來推進推薦系統。然而，整合它們的互補優勢仍然具有挑戰性，特別是在稀疏環境中，保持語義精確性至關重要。我們提出了TRWH（文本驅動隨機漫遊異構圖神經網絡），這是一個新穎的框架，通過戰略性隨機漫遊增強將LLM生成的文本檔案與異構圖結構融合。TRWH由三個核心組件組成：（1）嵌入創建，使用Word2Vec和基於LLM的檔案生成用戶和項目表示；（2）異構圖神經網絡（HeteroGNN），在多關係邊上傳播信息；以及（3）基於隨機漫遊的路徑構建，通過二階用戶-用戶和項目-項目鏈接豐富稀疏圖。對Amazon-2023時尚（200萬用戶，82.5萬項目）和美容（63.1萬用戶，11.2萬項目）數據集的實驗表明，TRWH在性能上顯著超越了最先進的方法，包括在時尚領域實現80.0%的RMSE和52.6%的MAE減少，以及在美容領域實現25.7%和10.8%的改進。值得注意的是，儘管隨機漫遊在傳統嵌入中提高了性能，但它們可能稀釋LLMs學習到的細緻表示，這突顯了自適應整合策略的重要性。

Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks.
